| 2026-10-16 | Tooling — faster `prisma generate` and script commands (**done**, no release) | `patches/prisma@7.10.0.patch` (pnpm `patchedDependencies`, applied by `pnpm install`): Prisma 7.10's client generator answers `typeNeedsGenericModelArg` with a per-type graph walk over all input types — quadratic on our 533 models (>17 min and out of memory). The patch computes the answer for every input type in one reverse pass over the field-reference graph and falls back to the original walk for anything else; the generated client is byte-identical. `prisma generate` ~2 min here (the rest is Prisma's own schema engine). `pnpm build:fast` (SWC, ~20 s, no type-check) replaces the full `tsc` `nest build` (~3 min, 6 GiB) before `db:seed*`, `rbac:build`, `launch:settings`, `release:check`, `styles:*`, `cms:home-photos`, `uat:styles`; `build` and `contract:build` unchanged; `typecheck` stays in CI. `.swcrc` keeps `import()` native (`module.ignoreDynamic`, as `tsc` emits it) so the vendored `@getpack/dieline` bundle loads from an SWC build (also `pnpm dev`). Outputs of `rbac:build` and `launch:settings` unchanged. `uat:styles` loads `.env` like the seed runner; on a published UAT database it takes ~22 s including the build (was ~3 min of `tsc` first). Units 1184. | — | Drop the patch when Prisma fixes the generator upstream (the patch fails to apply on another version, which `pnpm install` reports) | — |

(3) Staff roles can be granted only by someone who holds all of their permissions, and never by oneself: Admin (`roles.assign`) therefore cannot grant Finance, C-Level etc. — Superadmin does — **confirm**. (8) The OAuth2 token endpoint uses RFC 6749 snake_case fields (exception to the camelCase rule); its errors stay problem details. (9) OTP resend cool-down is per purpose (sign in, then link LINE at once); the hourly cap spans purposes. (10) `AUTH_LOGIN_THROTTLE_LIMIT` (env, default 10/min/IP) and the per-purpose file size ceilings are technical limits, not business values. (12) Notification delivery is at-least-once (LINE deduplicates by retry key; a crash between send and commit can repeat an e-mail).
